# Application Security and Development Security Technical Implementation Guide: suggested statuses

Provisional recommendations for the identified candidate/source snapshots. No live MPN assessment or formal sign-off. Evaluate each component listed separately before final checklists.

| Rule | Requirement | Suggested status | Justification | Evidence / next step |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V-222387 / APSC-DV-000010 | The application must provide a capability to limit the number of logon sessions per user. | Needs work | UDS includes a deny-new-session limiter, but actual Core default SSO_SESSION_MAX_PER_USER=0 is unlimited. The official production check asks that the organization-defined maximum actually be configured; provider capability resolves implementation ownership but does not choose/set that maximum. The existing October per-rule evidence/test notes are preserved as historical context only. | [re-asd-release](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon-uds-bundle/actions/runs/42114745685) [re-asd-realm](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-identity-config/blob/74480722e1cc8405b5e9b65ac7d7086ab7f4056d/src/realm.json5) [re-asd-core-id](https://github.com/defenseunicorns/uds-core/blob/c15677633cffa17e99656a7d7ccd1f98d7cb6e4d/src/keycloak/chart/values.yaml) Set the approved per-user maximum in UDS identity, or document an accepted unlimited policy/tailoring. No Horizon session-limiter code is needed. |
| V-222388 / APSC-DV-000060 | The application must clear temporary storage and cookies when the session is terminated. | Likely covered | Auth store explicitly wipes local user/session state and invokes IdP signout; a dedicated logged-out view presents termination feedback. Outstanding bearer-token revocation and browser-close persistence are separately flagged, rather than claiming logout is absent. The existing October per-rule evidence/test notes are preserved as historical context only. | [app-auth-service](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon/blob/3f3b51d454197c077b33896caf93de72b94eafda/apps/web/src/auth/auth-service.ts) [logout](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon/blob/3f3b51d454197c077b33896caf93de72b94eafda/apps/web/src/auth/auth-store.ts) [app-logged-out](https://github.com/gallatinai/horizon/blob/3f3b51d454197c077b33896caf93de72b94eafda/apps/web/src/views/LoggedOutView.vue) Retain this source mapping with the selected release; verify only deployment overrides, exemptions and applicability before final checklist sign-off. |

| V-222431 / APSC-DV-000520 | The application must audit the execution of privileged functions. | Needs work | Credit existing UDS identity/admin events, append-only actor-aware role grants/revocations and database change provenance. Remaining gap is selected app-domain successful/denied protected operations: the permission enforcer throws403 without emitting a structured actor/target/operation event, and generic HTTP logs omit actor. Central proxy identity records do not carry all tenant/object decisions. This is a targeted event-catalog/attribution integration, not an absence of auditing or a requirement to add actors to every diagnostic/request log. | V-222570 / APSC-DV-002020 | The application must utilize FIPS-validated cryptographic modules when signing application components. | Needs work | The actual app runtime crypto is covered by FIPS images, but the separate package signing path uses upstream static Go Witness0.12.0 with no GOFIPS140 build selection; the wrapper strips GODEBUG. This is a narrow release-signing-provider issue, not a missing application TLS feature.
